# GSQ224 representative confirmation: preserved completed cells and host stops

The frozen three-pair confirmation completes two full pairs, all with clean timing, physical bounds, correct coding answers and stable within-arm output tokens. It stops before launching the third original process because macOS Photos background activity prevents the required quiet interval. No failed timing outcome is replaced or removed.

After a fresh observation finds Photos idle, a prospectively frozen one-time continuation reuses all four completed cells, verifies their receipt identities, and attempts only the missing pair under unchanged thresholds. The original process completes with all gates intact. The last candidate preflight stops before model allocation: brief activity from the chat renderer and duetexpertd resets the continuous quiet interval, so the deadline expires. Five actual model runs now exist, three original and two candidate. The complete six-cell verdict remains absent. This is a host-readiness limitation, not a native failure or performance rejection.

All completed native receipts, preflight samples, stopped coordinators, frozen protocols and exact runner bytes follow. No system process is killed, paused or reconfigured. No partial favorable median becomes qualification. The original comparator is the strongest completed low-budget coding control, not a proven per-workload optimum. Four-draft GSQ and two-draft original remain distinct declared configurations of the same checkpoint.

The next fourteen-GB pilot is prepared but not frozen or launched: its preparation refuses until the six-cell confirmation is complete and passes. The separate prospective feature scope uses the existing owned-vision and full-context instruments with unchanged physical guards. It does not establish image-answer quality, speed, deployment or Auto eligibility. Source changes for explicit GSQ vision admission and truthful context receipt identity are being built separately; no new numerical result is asserted here.
